## User-Perspective Review: Convenience & Power (2026-03-15)

Review from the user's standpoint — how easily they can get started and how much they can accomplish.

### Convenience — Getting There

**Strengths:**
- **`dan-up`** — Single command to start server and drop into chat; zero-friction entry.
- **`dan-chat --local`** — Works without server; good for offline or quick one-offs.
- **Chat EmptyState** — Clear prompts ("Build a workflow", "Ask About Your Workflow") with one-click suggestions; mode-specific (agent/ask/plan/debug/auto).
- **Research QuickStartPanel** — 6 templates (Literature Review, New Paper, Review Paper, Compare Papers, Summarize Paper, Learn 100 Papers) with domain filtering.
- **README Quick Start** — Prerequisites, install, configure, run; `.env.example` with commented "Quick Profiles" bundle.
- **CLI reference** — `docs/cli.md` documents 11 commands with options and env vars.

**Friction points:**
- ~~**Two terminals for editor**~~ **FIXED 2026-03-15.** Added `dan-editor` CLI command that starts both server and editor dev server in one terminal.
- ~~**Quick Profiles off by default**~~ **FIXED 2026-03-15.** `DAN_FULL_TOOLS=1` now default (uncommented in `.env.example`, Python default flipped to on).
- **No single "first run" flow** — README shows Python workflow, editor, and CLI; no guided path for "I want to build X."
- ~~**Editor build fails**~~ **FIXED 2026-03-15.** 97 TS errors fixed; `npm run build` passes.
- **MCP optional** — `pip install -e ".[mcp]"` is separate; users may miss MCP tools if they don't read that section.

### Power — Doing Projects

**Strengths:**
- **Three authoring surfaces** — Python DSL, visual editor, markdown; all compile to same graph.
- **Natural-language goals** — `dan-run "Summarize the latest AI papers"`; MetaController plans and runs.
- **Chat as control plane** — Build, modify, run, publish, export from conversation; `/run`, `/show`, `/undo`, `/help`.
- **24+ capability tools** — Experience search, run lifecycle, graph listing; LLM picks by intent.
- **Multi-surface** — CLI, editor, Telegram, WhatsApp, gateway; shared run management.
- **Rich editor** — Multi-tab workflows, node palette, gates, live execution, human-in-the-loop, validation.
- **Research mode** — Writing pane, PDF reader, references, distillation, Furnace; domain profiles.

**Gaps / hard-to-reach power:**
- ~~**Learning/tools off**~~ **FIXED 2026-03-15.** `DAN_FULL_TOOLS` now defaults to on.
- **"Graph changed since your last message"** — Stale-revision warning can confuse; user may not know whether to dismiss or refresh.
- **Mode switching** — Chat/Code/Research/Operations; no clear guidance on when to switch or what each mode is for.
- **Research quick-starts dispatch to chat** — `dispatchResearchCommand` sends to persistent chat; if user is in Code mode, flow may feel disconnected.
- **No "run from here" in Research** — Quick-starts set pipeline stages and content but don't clearly show how to execute the full pipeline.
- **Workspace sandbox** — Tests expect `ValueError` for paths outside root; tools may not enforce. User could write outside workspace without realizing.
- ~~**`/cost` and `/retry`**~~ **NOTE 2026-03-15:** Command handlers never implemented; not a runtime bug.

### Recommendations (document only)

- Add a "first run" wizard or `dan init` that creates `.env` with Quick Profiles enabled and prints next steps.
- Document a single recommended path: "For visual workflow building: `dan-serve` + `cd editor && npm run dev`" with `dan-up` as the chat-only alternative.
- Surface Quick Profile toggles in the editor (e.g. Settings) so users can enable learning/full tools without editing `.env`.
- Fix editor build so `npm run build` and Electron packaging work for distribution.
- Add a mode-switch hint or tooltip: "Code = files/terminal, Research = papers/writing, Chat = workflow authoring."

### Summary

- **Convenience:** Good — `dan-up`, chat empty states, quick-starts. Friction: two terminals for editor, Quick Profiles off by default, no guided first run.
- **Power:** Strong — NL goals, multi-surface, rich editor, research mode. Gaps: power gated by env, mode switching unclear, some CLI commands broken (`/cost`, `/retry`).

## Main Mechanisms & Key Modules Review (2026-03-15)

Review of core mechanisms and how they work together in tandem.

### Core Data Flows

**Chat → Concierge → Graph/Run:**
- `ChatManager` holds `graph_store`, `capability_context`; `CapabilityContext` has `graph_store`, `run_manager`, `chat_manager`, etc.
- Concierge dispatches to tier executors; tier executors call `chat_manager.send_message_with_tools()` directly.
- Capability handlers receive `CapabilityContext` with workflow_id, graph_store, run_manager.
- **Works well:** Handlers can load graphs, start runs, list experiences; chat gets graph context for prompts.

**Gateway → Planner:**
- `gateway/router.py` calls `planner.execute_plan(plan, user_text=goal)` and expects `exec_result.get("graph")`.
- `execute_plan` delegates to `execute_plan_direct` for `GenerateCodePlan` when `DAN_DIRECT_BUILD` is on.
- `execute_plan_direct` returns `{"workflow_id": ..., "graph_data": graph_data, ...}` — **uses `graph_data` not `graph`**.
- **Broken:** Gateway gets `None` for `graph` when direct build path is used; raises 422.

**Run Events → Chat Handoff:**
- `chat_complete` can carry `stream_channel_id` for a `run-*` channel; client reconnects to that channel for run output.
- `chat_message` handler: `/run` parses → `RunManager.start_run` → `_pipe_run_events` subscribes to run queue, pushes to `run-*` stream.
- **Works:** Run events flow from RunManager to chat stream; client sees both chat completion and run progress.

### Event Routing

**Two WebSocket paths:**
1. **Main WS** (`/ws`): `wsConnection` connects here; `eventRouter` subscribes to `wsConnection.on("*")`.
2. **Chat stream WS** (`/api/chat/{channel}/events`): Dedicated per chat/run channel; chat tokens, tool results, run events.

**Research mode events:**
- `eventRouter` routes `artifact_created`, `node_started`, `node_completed` → `dan:engine-event-raw` CustomEvents (only when `activeMode === "research"`).
- `useResearchEvents` listens for `dan:engine-event-raw` and bridges to `researchEventRouter` → research store.
- **Source:** Main WS receives events from `GlobalEventBus` / run engine broadcasts. Chat stream events are not forwarded to main WS.
- **Gap:** If a workflow run is started from chat (e.g. `/run` or build-then-run), run events go to the chat stream WS, not the main WS. Research mode would not receive `node_started`/`node_completed` for pipeline stage updates unless the backend also broadcasts to the main WS.

**Mode sync:** `useEventRouter` syncs `activeMode` from `useAppStore` to `eventRouter.setActiveMode()`. Mode switching is correct.

### Research QuickStart ↔ Backend

**Flow:**
- QuickStart actions call `dispatchResearchCommand("/research literature-review \"topic\"")` → `persistent-chat:send` CustomEvent.
- `PersistentChatBar` listens and sends to chat API.
- Backend receives plain text; no `/research` command handler exists — concierge treats it as natural language.
- QuickStart also sets `store.setPrimaryTab("editor")`, `store.togglePipeline()`, etc. — local UI state only.

**Gaps:**
- Pipeline stages in Research UI are local state; they do not auto-sync with backend run progress unless the backend emits `node_started`/`node_completed` to the main WS.
- No dedicated research workflow type; "literature review" is a conversational request that may or may not produce a structured workflow.
- Research events from `researchEventRouter` (artifact_created, distillation_progress) expect backend to emit; if those come from chat-stream-only paths, Research mode won't see them.

### CapabilityContext & Handlers

**Shared context:** `CapabilityContext` is built at startup; per-request copies are created with `workflow_id` set. Handlers have `graph_store`, `run_manager`, `chat_manager`, `event_bus`, etc.

**Works:** Run lifecycle, experience search, graph listing, publish, introspection — all wired.

**Missing:** `event_bus` is optional; if handlers emit to a bus that the main WS consumes, Research mode could receive events. Need to verify whether capability handlers emit to the main bus or only to chat stream.

### UX Improvement Areas (from mechanism review)

1. ~~**Gateway direct-build:**~~ **FIXED 2026-03-15.** `execute_plan_direct` now returns `"graph"`.
2. ~~**Research pipeline sync:**~~ **FIXED 2026-03-15.** Chat stream WS handler in `ChatPanel.tsx` now bridges `node_started`, `node_completed`, `artifact_created` events as `dan:engine-event-raw` CustomEvents so Research mode receives them.
3. **Event source clarity:** Document which events come from main WS vs chat stream; ensure Research mode receives pipeline events when runs originate from chat.
4. **Research command:** Optional `/research` command could set structured pipeline + dispatch to a research-specific handler for clearer UX.
5. **CapabilityContext.event_bus:** Verify handlers emit to the bus that main WS consumers receive; if not, Research pipeline UI stays disconnected from chat-originated runs.

### Summary

- **Chat ↔ Concierge ↔ Graph/Run:** ✓ Works well.
- ~~**Gateway ↔ Planner:** ✗ Direct build returns `graph_data`; gateway expects `graph`.~~ **FIXED 2026-03-15.**
- ~~**Event routing:** Research mode may not receive run events when runs start from chat.~~ **FIXED 2026-03-15.** Chat stream events bridged to research router.
- **Research QuickStart:** Sends to chat; pipeline stages are local but now receive backend events via bridge.

## User Interactions Review (2026-03-15)

Focus on input affordances, feedback, error recovery, and discoverability.

### Input Surfaces

**Chat input:**
- **Chat mode:** Full `ChatPanel` with textarea, Send, Stop, Queue, Voice (fullScreen), attachments (drop zone), mode selector, thread tabs.
- **PersistentChatBar (non-Chat modes):** Single-line textarea, Send, Cmd+J to expand; placeholder "Ask DAN anything… (⌘J to expand)". Sends via `persistent-chat:send` → switches to Chat mode.
- **ChatSidebar (Code mode):** Separate lightweight chat with "Ask about your code…" placeholder.

~~**Potential race:**~~ **FIXED 2026-03-15.** PersistentChatBar now writes pending message to `useAppStore.pendingChatMessage` instead of firing a CustomEvent. ChatPanel checks this on mount and sends it.

~~**PersistentChatBar Paperclip:**~~ **FIXED 2026-03-15.** Dead Paperclip button removed.

### Feedback & Loading States

**Strengths:**
- Chat: Typing indicator (thinking/executing), Stop button when streaming, Queue strip with Push/Edit/Remove for queued messages.
- Error banner with Retry button; stale-revision banner with Dismiss.
- Build-just-completed: "Workflow built successfully" + "Run this workflow" CTA.
- Plan mode: Approve/Revise buttons after assistant plan.
- Escalation banner with mode switch + preference (always/ask/never).

**Gaps:**
- ~~Stale-revision: "Dismiss" only hides; no "Refresh context" or "Retry with current graph".~~ **FIXED 2026-03-15.** Added "Retry with current" button with RotateCcw icon.
- Error retry: `retryLast` re-sends last user message; no indication of what will be retried.
- Loading states: Some panels (CallHierarchy, DebugPanel, QuickOpen) show loading; others (e.g. Research QuickStart dispatch) give no feedback that the message was sent or is processing.

### Error Recovery

**Strengths:**
- ErrorBoundary: "Something went wrong" + "Try again" (resets error state).
- GraphDiffPreview: "Try again" on failed apply.
- Chat: Retry on error; reconnect logic for stream drops.
- HumanInputDialog: Submit (Cmd+Enter) with loading state.

**Gaps:**
- ~~No global "connection lost" or "backend unavailable" banner when API/WS fails across the app.~~ **FIXED 2026-03-15.** Added `ConnectionBanner` component to `AppShell` — polls `/api/health`, shows amber "Backend unavailable — reconnecting…" banner, green flash on reconnect.
- ToastContainer for editor actions (import, publish, etc.) but chat errors use inline banners — inconsistent.
- ~~PersistentChatBar: No feedback if send fails.~~ **FIXED 2026-03-15.** Now uses store-based message passing (not events); Chat mount picks it up reliably.

### Discoverability

**Strengths:**
- ModeBar: Tooltips with shortcuts (⌘1, ⌘2, …).
- Chat input hint: "Enter to send · Shift+Enter for newline · ⌘K command palette".
- GlobalCommandPalette (⌘K): Commands with shortcuts.
- KeybindingsPanel: Searchable shortcuts.
- EmptyState: One-click prompt suggestions per mode.

**Gaps:**
- Cmd+J (PersistentChatBar expand): Not in GlobalCommandPalette; only in placeholder. Users in Code/Research may not discover it.
- ~~Slash commands (/run, /show, /undo, /help, /research…): No in-UI list or autocomplete.~~ **FIXED 2026-03-15.** Added `SlashCommandPopup` — dropdown appears when user types `/` with 7 commands and descriptions.
- Mode switching: No hint on first visit that ⌘1–6 switch modes; ModeBar labels don't say "press ⌘2 for Research".
- Research QuickStart: Dispatches to chat but user is switched away from Research UI; may not see the response unless they notice the mode change.

### Confirmation & Destructive Actions

**Strengths:**
- Graph delete: `confirm("Delete ...?")`.
- Mutation preview: Apply/Reject with diff.
- Plan mode: Explicit Approve before mutation.

**Gaps:**
- ~~Some `confirm()` dialogs are native browser modals — not styled; inconsistent with app.~~ **FIXED 2026-03-15.** Created reusable `ConfirmDialog` component; replaced native `confirm()` in EditorToolbar, GraphSwitcher, and ChatPanel thread delete.
- ~~No undo for graph delete or thread delete.~~ **FIXED 2026-03-15.** Added soft-delete with 8-second undo toast for graph and thread deletion. Extended toast system with action buttons.

### Recommendations (document only)

1. ~~**PersistentChatBar:**~~ **FIXED.** Store-based pending message.
2. ~~**Paperclip:**~~ **FIXED.** Removed.
3. ~~**Stale-revision:**~~ **FIXED.** "Retry with current" button added.
4. ~~**Discoverability:**~~ **PARTIALLY FIXED.** Slash-command popup added. Cmd+J already in palette.
5. ~~**Cross-mode send feedback:**~~ **FIXED.** Store-based message passing ensures Chat mode receives it on mount.

### Summary

- **Input:** Multiple surfaces (Chat, PersistentChatBar, ChatSidebar). ~~Race and Paperclip~~ fixed.
- **Feedback:** Good typing/queue/error UI. ~~Stale-revision~~ now has Retry button.
- **Discoverability:** Mode shortcuts, EmptyState, ~~slash-command popup~~ added.
- **Recovery:** ErrorBoundary, chat retry, ~~global ConnectionBanner~~ added. Store-based cross-mode send.

## Phase 33 Eval Findings (2026-03-11)

### Root causes (33-5 triage)
- **Routing (fixed):** CONFIRM handler and META_GOAL classification blocked explicit build requests. Fixed via `_requested_mode_forces_solver_path` bypass and META_GOAL→WORKFLOW_BUILD override.
- **LLM API flakiness (open):** Empty classifier responses, codegen timeouts, internal errors. Primary cause of cross-run variance. Retry (33-8) helps but provider stability is external.
- **Server stability (open):** 0-event responses under sustained eval load; WebSocket keepalive timeout on long codegen. `--delay` and heartbeat mitigate.
- **Semantic underspec (33-7):** Structurally valid but useless graphs (p02: 2-node review loop, p08: 1-node equity). Quality gates now flag score <30.

### LLM classifier returns empty response
- **Symptom:** `LLM classifier returned unparseable response: ` in server.log — empty string
- **Root cause:** The micro-tier classifier model (MiniMax-M2.5 via custom API endpoint) returns empty responses, possibly due to API issues or model configuration
- **Impact:** All prompts fall through to keyword heuristic classifier, which has limited pattern coverage
- **Workaround:** Added classification override for explicit `mode=build` requests
- **Status:** Open — classifier model/API reliability is outside eval harness scope

### Server solver path hangs for 60s+ without producing graph
- **Symptom:** After classification succeeds and routes to solver, the server emits progress-ack pings but never produces a final response or graph mutation
- **Root cause:** The solver path makes LLM calls (via the configured API endpoint) that appear to hang or timeout silently
- **Impact:** 0% graph creation rate in pilot evaluation (first run); improved to 60% after routing fixes
- **Workaround:** Added 90s wall-clock timeout in eval runner
- **Status:** Open for some prompts — likely LLM API reliability/latency issue

### `ActionPolicy.CONFIRM` blocks solver path for explicit build requests
- **Symptom:** Complex "Build a multi-department..." prompts get classified as `META_GOAL` (estimated cost 2.0 > threshold 1.0), triggering CONFIRM handler that returns "Please confirm before I do that" — BEFORE the solver-path bypass runs
- **Root cause:** CONFIRM check at line 1979 executes before `_requested_mode_forces_solver_path()` at line 2036
- **Fix applied:** Added `not _requested_mode_forces_solver_path(msg.metadata)` to CONFIRM guard; extended classification override to also catch `META_GOAL` → `WORKFLOW_BUILD` for explicit build mode
- **Status:** Fixed

### `normalize_chat_mode("build")` maps to `"agent"`, losing mode signal
- **Symptom:** `CHAT_MODE_ALIASES = {"build": "agent", "mutate": "agent"}` means the concierge never sees the original requested mode
- **Fix applied:** Added `requested_mode` field to SurfaceMessage metadata preserving the pre-normalization mode
- **Status:** Fixed

### ~~Build tools gated off despite workflow-build intent ("I don't have the tools")~~
- **Symptom:** In agent mode, the chat assistant says "I don't have the tools to build or modify workflows" and only exposes catalog/run tools. Mutation tool and codegen path both unavailable.
- **Root cause (three mismatches):**
  1. `allow_mutation_tool` in `tier_executors.py` was only set when `"workflow_edit" in required_action_hints`, but `_determine_stage()` treated turns as `workflow_build` more broadly (`mode == "build"` or `route_target == "workflow"`). A turn could be staged as workflow build without the mutation tool.
  2. `_ACTION_HINT_TOOL_MAP` in `chat/helpers.py` had no `workflow_edit` entry, so even when the hint was present, `tool_choice` stayed `"auto"` and the model could answer in prose.
  3. `_WORKFLOW_EDIT_RE` in `triage.py` required both a workflow-entity word AND an edit verb. Build/retry phrases like "retry to build" had no entity word, so the fallback never produced `workflow_edit`.
  4. `EMPTY_GRAPH_SUMMARY_PLACEHOLDER` still referenced `plan_graph_mutations` while the runtime now prefers the codegen path.
- **Fix applied (33-5 Cycle 4, tasks 16-19):** Widened `allow_mutation_tool` gate to match `_determine_stage` predicate; added `workflow_edit` → `plan_graph_mutations` to `_ACTION_HINT_TOOL_MAP`; broadened `_WORKFLOW_EDIT_RE` with build/retry verbs and added linked-workflow bridging; removed stale prompt references. Review also found `_WORKFLOW_EDIT_RE` lacked `try\s+again` (multi-word) even though `_WORKFLOW_BUILD_VERB_RE` had it, so the bridging path set `workflow_context_like = True` but the guard silently failed — added the pattern. A second follow-up fix moved workflow-edit reinforcement into triage post-processing, so successful but weak embedding/LLM routes (for example `ask/general` on "can you retry and fix this?") are now upgraded to `workflow_edit` instead of only getting the stronger hint on fallback parse failures.
- **Status:** Fixed (2026-03-17)

## Known Limitations (Adapter)

- ~~**Telegram fleet could re-process bot-authored group messages and sometimes drop final replies.**~~ **FIXED 2026-03-10.** Two related issues: (1) in group chats, Telegram could surface bot-authored messages in shapes where the fleet did not see `from_user.is_bot=True`, so a bot's own outgoing greeting/answer could be re-consumed and re-dispatched, causing repeated "Hi again..." loops; (2) final reply delivery could fail with `telegram.error.BadRequest: Message to be replied not found` when the original message was no longer replyable, aborting the dispatch. Fixed by hardening fleet-side bot-author detection using known fleet bot usernames plus bot IDs, by pre-seeding the fleet dedup cache with outbound Telegram `message_id`s so echoed bot sends are dropped even if Telegram omits author metadata, and by retrying Telegram sends once without `reply_to_message_id` when that specific error occurs.
- ~~**Starting two Telegram fleet processes could trigger duplicate replies and `getUpdates` 409 conflicts.**~~ **FIXED 2026-03-10.** A second `dan-bot start-all` process could poll the same bot tokens concurrently, so Telegram would terminate one poller with `Conflict: terminated by other getUpdates request`, while users could still see duplicate replies during the overlap window. Fixed by adding a single-instance fleet startup lock at `~/.dan/telegram/fleet.lock`, stale-lock recovery, and a clean CLI error instead of allowing the second fleet to start.
- ~~**Telegram adapter spammed Bot API 400s for command registration and reactions.**~~ **FIXED 2026-03-10.** Two noisy-but-nonfatal adapter issues: (1) Telegram command registration was forwarding slash commands with hyphens (for example `/goal-status`), which `setMyCommands` rejects because Telegram command names must match `[a-z0-9_]{1,32}`; (2) chats that reject bot-set reactions would trigger the same `setMessageReaction` `400 Bad Request` on every status update. Fixed by filtering Telegram command menus to Telegram-safe names only, and by disabling reactions per chat after the first Telegram `BadRequest`.
- ~~**Telegram fleet dedup treated the same human group message as four different messages across bots.**~~ **FIXED 2026-03-10.** Fresh live traces showed the same user message hitting `scholar`, `engineer`, `quant`, and `chief` at the same instant, but each bot saw a different local `message_id`. Because fleet dedup only keyed on `(chat_id, message_id)`, all four callbacks survived dedup, all four routed to the default `chief` winner, and the group got four delayed `chief` replies plus four reaction attempts. Fixed by adding a bot-agnostic inbound dedup fingerprint for non-private Telegram messages while keeping the per-message-id cache for outbound bot echoes.
- ~~**Telegram fleet serialized all private-DM turns for a bot behind one chat-wide history lock.**~~ **FIXED 2026-03-10.** The fleet used `_conversation_thread_key(chat_id, thread_id, bot)` as both the server continuity key and the adapter-side history lock key. In private chats `thread_id` is always `None`, so every DM to the same bot collapsed to `chat_id:main:bot` and the `async with history_lock:` block held that lock across the full server request + streaming lifecycle. Result: unrelated private messages could sit behind one long-running turn even though the shared concierge dispatcher already knows how to queue same-project work and parallelize independent work. Reviewed the working WhatsApp Web path, which forwards inbound turns without an adapter-wide execution lock. Fixed by keeping the broad conversation key for server-side continuity, but splitting Telegram's local history/lock scope into reply-aware lanes: forum topics and non-private chats still use the chat/topic key, replies reuse the prior lane, and unrelated private-DM messages get independent lanes so they can start in parallel.
- ~~**Messaging surfaces could treat `progress_ack` as the final answer and show awkward `"Got it. <user text>"` echoes.**~~ **FIXED 2026-03-10.** The runtime emitted instant acknowledgments as `ChatCompleteEvent(detected_mode="progress_ack")`, but several consumers still treated any `chat_complete` as terminal. Result: some adapter collectors stopped before the real answer arrived, while other bridges forwarded the ack as if it were the final response. Fixed by treating `progress_ack` as non-terminal in shared stream consumers (`cli/adapter.py`, Telegram fleet non-streaming collector, `dan-chat`) and by suppressing standalone relay of `progress_ack` in `app.py`'s adapter bridge. Telegram/messaging instant-ack copy is now generic `"Working on it..."` instead of echoing the user prompt.
- ~~**RunHandler starts useless no-op runs on empty adapter graphs.**~~ **FIXED 2026-03-09.** When a user said "run it" via WhatsApp, the classifier routed to `RUN_CONTROL`, which started a workflow run on an empty graph — completing in 0.02s with zero tokens and no output. The user got "[DAN] Run started: run-..." but nothing useful happened. Fixed by: (1) `handle_start_run` now rejects empty graphs (no nodes) with a clear message, (2) `RunHandler` now falls back to `DirectTaskHandler` (LLM+tools) when `start_run` fails, giving the user a real AI response. Also reset the malformed `_adapter_swhatsappnet` graph (had orphan nodes, missing output ports on for_each sub-graph `summarize` node).

- ~~**WhatsApp responses contained raw HTML tags.**~~ **FIXED 2026-03-09.** `web_fetch` returned raw HTML from web pages, the LLM included it in responses, and the adapter sent it verbatim to WhatsApp. Fixed by: converting HTML to text in `web_fetch`, adding `strip_html_for_messaging()` to the adapter output path, and strengthening surface hints to forbid HTML.

- ~~**MutationPlan crashes when LLM nests `add_edge` fields inside `config`.**~~ **FIXED 2026-03-09.** LLM sometimes generates `{"op": "add_edge", "config": {"source_id": ..., "target_id": ...}}` instead of flat fields. `_normalize_generated_mutation_ops` didn't normalize `add_edge` ops. User saw "I hit an internal error" with a Pydantic ValidationError. Fixed by lifting edge fields from `config` to top level in the normalizer.

- ~~**Chat auto-mode detection crashes with `AttributeError: 'dict' object has no attribute 'graph_id'`.**~~ **FIXED 2026-03-09.** `_produce()` in `app.py` used attribute access on dict objects from `list_runs()`. Every `mode="auto"` request crashed instantly (15ms). Fixed to use dict `.get()` access.

- ~~**Auto-mode picked the wrong run status when multiple runs existed for one workflow.**~~ **FIXED 2026-03-10.** Both server and local chat were checking the first matching run from `RunManager.list_runs()` rather than the newest one, so an older run could incorrectly force or suppress `debug` mode. Fixed by adding shared `recent_run_failed_for_workflow()` logic in `chat_manager.py` that selects the latest matching run by `started_at` and supports both dict snapshots and older object-style entries. Covered by focused regressions in `tests/test_server/test_auto_mode.py` and `tests/test_server/test_chat_local_auto_mode.py`.

- ~~**WhatsApp paper summary returned raw PDF text.**~~ **FIXED 2026-03-09.** Two root causes: (1) `FileHandler._review_document` embedded 12,000 chars of raw PDF text in the user message and called `send_message` (no tools), but `UNIFIED_SYSTEM_PROMPT` rule #2 says "NEVER summarize a file you haven't read — call pdf_read first." Since no tools were available, the LLM was confused and could output the raw text verbatim. (2) `SURFACE_HINTS` for WhatsApp mentioned "never include raw HTML from web_fetch" but said nothing about pdf_read text. Fixed by: (a) `_review_document` now strips the adapter-generated "Please review this PDF: /path" prefix and "[User sent file: ...]" lines via `_extract_user_intent()`, and uses truthful "full text" vs. "truncated excerpt" wording; (b) `ChatManager.send_message()` / `_build_messages()` now support `extra_system_instructions`, and the WhatsApp PDF-review path uses a system-priority override that inline document text already counts as read source material; (c) `SURFACE_HINTS` for `whatsapp` and `whatsapp-web` now add "Never reproduce raw extracted text from pdf_read or file_read — always produce a structured summary or answer." Covered by focused regressions in `tests/test_server/test_whatsapp_document_review.py`.

- **Adapter heuristic classifier is keyword-only.** `_classify_adapter_intent()` uses keyword matching, not LLM intent classification. It handles common patterns (file requests, continuations, workflow build) but will miss nuanced intent (e.g. "remember that analysis we did last month" should probably search experience, but routes to conversation). The full fix is 25-6 (intent dispatcher with LLM fallback).
- **Adapter file search is filename-only, not content-aware.** `_search_local_files()` now normalizes separators (`space`/`_`/`-`) and ranks token matches, so natural-language queries match filenames more reliably, but it still searches filenames only. It will not find a file based purely on content inside the document.

- **LLM fabricates live data (prices, stats) in ask/conversation mode.** When a message doesn't match the keyword classifier for `direct_task` (e.g., "close price" instead of "stock price"), it routes to `conversation` → `ask` mode, which is text-only with no tools. The LLM then invents plausible-looking data from stale training knowledge. Historical stop-gap was an ask-mode honesty guard; the current structural direction is plan [25-12](plans/25-12-tool-aware-conversation.md) plus the unified prompt/text-only override path so unsupported live-data requests are answered honestly instead of fabricated.

## Known Limitations (Phase 15)

- **`dan-chat --local` still lacks real `meta_goal` delegation.** The local chat service factory builds a concierge without `meta_controller`, so `MetaGoalHandler` returns `"Meta controller not available."` in local mode even though server-backed concierge can delegate open-ended goals. Fix requires passing `meta_controller` through local chat service construction.
- **Multi-tool dispatch doesn't re-call LLM.** When the LLM returns multiple capability tool calls, results are formatted directly to the user without a second LLM round-trip for synthesis. Acceptable for V1 (low latency), but complex multi-tool scenarios may produce mechanical output.
- **Messaging adapter chat bridge is wired, but mutation-apply UX is incomplete on messaging surfaces.** Telegram/WhatsApp/email adapters can now route messages through `ChatManager` in chat mode, but when a mutation is proposed and `auto_approve=False` there is not yet a first-class follow-up command path that stores and applies the pending mutation cleanly. Today the bridge works best for read/query/run flows or auto-approve mode; reviewed mutation confirmation over messaging still needs a dedicated follow-up flow.
- **Concierge clarification fallback is still partial.** The runtime now persists pending confirmation/clarification state and can resume on `"yes"`, `"no"`, numeric selections, or a text match against an option. If the follow-up is still ambiguous after one round, it now auto-picks the first ranked option and proceeds; remaining gap: the fallback still relies on handler-provided option ordering instead of an explicit confidence score, and it does not add a true undo flow.
- **Meta-goal delegation starts sessions, but full meta-session streaming is still thin.** `meta_goal` now opens a real `MetaSession` through `MetaController`, links it to the project, and keeps the task active. Remaining gap: the concierge currently returns an immediate "meta session started" acknowledgment rather than streaming stage-by-stage meta events back to the surface.
- **Shared concierge file handling does not yet own surface-specific delivery.** The shared `FileHandler` can search and rank matches, but actual attachment/file-send behavior still lives in adapter-local wrappers. Server/local concierge flows return matched paths as text today; full attachment-aware follow-ups remain to be unified.
- **CLI `_bg_reader` and readline concurrency (resolved 2026-03-06).** Historical issue where background stdin reads could race main-thread `input()` (notably during `human_input_needed` prompts). Fixed with stdin lock + pause coordination in `dan-chat`; keep this note to avoid regressing the same pattern.

## Error Memory Migration Notes

When renaming workflows or moving error data between environments:

- **Collection naming**: `ErrorMemoryIndex` stores errors in vector collections named `dan_errors_{workflow_id}`. If a workflow is renamed (e.g. `_scratch` → `equity-research`), its error history stays under the old collection name. To migrate: export errors from old collection, re-index under the new name.
- **Global vs workflow scope**: `ErrorMemoryIndex.query()` with `scope="global"` scans ALL `dan_errors_*` collections. `scope="workflow"` searches only `dan_errors_{workflow_id}`. The `PrincipleStore` works similarly — `scope="global"` scans all workflow subdirectories in the memory store.
- **Cross-environment migration**: Error collections live in the vector store (memory/FAISS/Chroma). To move error history between machines: export the vector store data directory and the `memory/` filesystem store directory together. They are separate storage backends but both keyed by `workflow_id`.
- **Principle deduplication**: `PrincipleStore.load_principles(scope="global")` de-duplicates by principle `id` and keeps the highest-confidence version. If the same principle is stored under multiple workflow IDs, global scope merges them correctly.

## Resolved Bugs

- **[26-6-A5] Adapter chat mode could emit double DAN labels on messaging surfaces:** Adapter-local chat-mode replies in `src/dan/cli/adapter.py` still had hardcoded `[DAN] ...` strings for file/send/search responses, while server-originated replies were also being label-stripped separately. This could surface as duplicated labels like `[DAN] [DAN] Sent: ...` depending on the adapter/rendering path. Fixed by centralizing the prefix into a shared `_DAN_PREFIX` constant plus `_strip_dan_prefix()` helper and removing hardcoded local DAN labels from adapter-managed prompts. Added focused regressions for shared prefix stripping and `/send` replies without the prefix.
- **[26-6-A4] Adapter chat mode hid DAN server outages behind a generic fallback message:** `dan-adapter` chat mode sent every natural-language message through a raw `httpx.AsyncClient(...).post("/api/chat/message")`. When `dan-serve` was not reachable, the resulting `httpx.ConnectError` was caught only by the broad outer exception handler, which logged a traceback and replied with `config.error_message` (`"Something went wrong. Please try again."`). Fixed by catching server connectivity failures explicitly in chat mode and replying with a clear server-unavailable message that includes the DAN server URL. Added a regression test covering the unreachable-server path.
- **[25-7-R1] Messaging-surface mutation defaults were bypassed on the shared concierge path:** `/api/chat/message` always created `SurfaceMessage(surface="server", ...)`, so WhatsApp/Telegram/email traffic lost the original surface identity before `resolve_policy()` ran. Fixed by adding `surface` to the chat request schema, forwarding it from adapter/CLI clients, and carrying request history into concierge metadata so surface-specific system context can survive the server handoff.
- **[26-6-A3] WhatsApp Web document sends omitted MIME type for `.docx`:** `WhatsAppWebAdapter.send_file()` sent the original document bytes via `neonize.send_document()` with `filename=...` but without `mimetype=...`. For OOXML formats like `.docx`, the raw bytes look like a ZIP container, so the receiving client could label it as a zip/generic document instead of the original Word file type. Fixed by adding `_guess_mimetype()` and passing the explicit MIME for document sends.
- **[26-6-A2] Adapter `/find` missed real files when query words used spaces but filenames used underscores/hyphens:** `_handle_find_command()` built a literal recursive glob like `**/*late payment seasonality*`, so `/Users/lizhi/Dropbox/late_payment_seasonality_20251106.docx` was invisible to the matcher even though all tokens were present in order. Fixed by replacing literal glob matching with `_search_local_files()` normalized token search + ranking, and reusing the same helper for `_handle_send_command()` fallback resolution.
- **[26-6-A1] Polite adapter file requests fell through to fake tool-call text:** The quick adapter classifier only matched direct imperative prefixes like `send me ...`, so real chat phrasing such as `"Good. Can you send me the late payment seasonality doc file under ~/Dropbox folder?"` was classified as `conversation` and forwarded to server `ask` mode. At the same time, `ChatManager._build_messages()` still appended `CAPABILITY_TOOLS_REFERENCE` in `ask` mode even though the text-only path does not pass executable tools, so some models emitted textual tool-call markup (for example `<minimax:tool_call>`) with fabricated paths. Fixed by adding polite-send query extraction on the adapter side and only injecting capability-tool guidance in non-`ask`/`plan` modes.
- **[24-4-H1] Diagnosis port auto-fix not triggered for real validator port errors:** Validation messages like `has no output/input port ... (available: [...])` were classified as `edge_endpoint`, but auto-fix only handled `port_conflict`/`missing_port`, so deterministic port-rename repair did not run on common cases. Fixed by extending strategy selection and auto-fix application to `edge_endpoint` port messages.
- **[24-4-H2] Diagnosis loop could dead-end on auto-fix without fallback:** If strategy selected `auto_fix` but no deterministic patch could be inferred, the attempt failed and retried with the same strategy. Fixed by degrading to focused `re_prompt` within the same attempt when `llm_complete` is available.
- **[24-4-H3] Port auto-fix replacement scope was too broad:** Replacing quoted strings globally could rewrite unrelated literals outside edge/port wiring expressions. Fixed by constraining rewrites to known port contexts (`.port(...)`, `source_port`/`target_port`, and `connect(...)` port arguments).
- **[24-4-H4] Quote-style variance reduced diagnosis extraction reliability:** Several regexes assumed single-quoted IDs/ports, so double-quoted validator/build messages could skip artifact/port inference. Fixed by accepting both single- and double-quoted forms in diagnosis parsing heuristics.
- **[24-3-H1] Quality-suite mismatch taxonomy was too coarse:** Structure/topology/round-trip failures were collapsed into one error type, reducing triage precision in reports. Fixed by emitting explicit mismatch types (`topology_mismatch`, `structure_mismatch`, `round_trip_mismatch`, `multiple_mismatch`).

- **[21-6-C4] Mutation apply always fails with stale revision (`"Plan based on revision X but current is Y"`):** `compute_graph_revision()` hashed the raw dict read from disk (e.g. `{nodes, edges, metadata}`), but `build_graph_summary` in ChatManager round-tripped through `Graph.model_validate()` + `model_dump_json()` before hashing. Pydantic adds defaults (`version`, `sub_graphs`, `entry_points`, `exit_points`, `shared_context`, `artifact_refs`, `hyperedges`, expanded `metadata` with `name`, `description`, `created_at`, `tags`), producing a completely different JSON and therefore a different SHA-256 prefix. The plan's `base_graph_revision` used the Pydantic-enriched hash; the apply endpoint used the raw hash. They could never match. Fixed by normalizing through `Graph.model_validate()` inside `compute_graph_revision()`.
- **[21-6-C6] LLM mutation plans fail because node IDs don't match (`"Node 'node_1' not found"`):** `AddNode` had no `id` field -- the mutator auto-generated IDs by slugifying names (e.g. `"Research Input"` -> `"research-input"`). But the LLM used sequential IDs like `node_1`, `node_2` in `set_position` and `add_edge` ops. These never matched the slugified IDs. Fixed by: (a) adding optional `id` field to `AddNode` model + tool schema so LLM can specify stable IDs; (b) building a sequential alias table (`node_1` -> `research-input`, `node_2` -> `data-gathering`, ...) during plan application so old-style `node_N` references resolve automatically. Both `_apply_all_or_nothing` and `_apply_partial` resolve aliases before each operation.
- **[21-6-C7] Input-node aggregate port mismatch (`"Source node 'node_input' has no output port 'input'"`):** Generated plans often wire from `node_input.input`, but variable-based Input nodes exposed only variable outputs (`ticker`, `company_name`, etc.) and no aggregate `input` output, so apply failed during source-port validation. Fixed by: (a) mutator default ports now keep variable outputs and also include aggregate `input`; (b) add-edge compatibility path auto-creates missing `input` output on existing Input nodes; (c) Input executor emits `outputs["input"]` as dict of resolved input variables.
- **[21-6-C8] Build-mode forced strict edges rejected semantic target ports:** Chat build mode forced `strict=True` on all generated `add_edge` ops via `_coerce_strict_edges`. This blocked plans that intentionally route multiple semantic inputs into a node (`fundamental_analysis`, `sentiment_analysis`, `consensus_analysis`, `recommendation`) because those ports are usually created on the fly. Fixed by changing `_coerce_strict_edges` to preserve generated strictness instead of forcing `strict=True`.
- **[21-6-C9] CLI prompted apply even when dry-run already failed:** `dan-chat` still asked `Apply mutation? [Y/n]` after `chat_mutation` events with `dry_run_result.success == false`, leading users into predictable `apply` failures and repeated loops. Fixed by `_should_prompt_apply()` guard in CLI: failed dry-run plans are not prompt-applied; user gets an explicit repair follow-up message.
- **[21-6-I6] Auto-repair only tried one correction attempt:** Tool-calling path had a single retry after dry-run failure, which often wasn't enough for large generated plans. Fixed by a bounded multi-attempt retry loop (`DAN_MUTATION_AUTO_RETRY_MAX`, default 2) that iteratively re-prompts with latest errors and keeps the newest attempted plan/result.
- **[21-6-C10] Generated mutation configs failed Graph parse for `parallel_subagents` and `validator`:** LLM frequently emitted near-miss shapes (`branch_graphs` as dict instead of list; `branch_inputs` values as strings instead of dicts; validator `rule_type="required_field"` instead of `required_keys`). Dry-run failed with Pydantic parse errors before any apply. Fixed by `_normalize_generated_mutation_ops()` in `chat_manager` to coerce these common schema drifts before building `MutationPlan`.
- **[21-6-C11] Generated `edge_type: context` in chat plans caused Graph parse errors:** Chat mutation `add_edge` ops do not include `ContextEdge` fields (`context_key`, `mode`), so when the model emits `edge_type="context"`, resulting edges are schema-invalid (`Field required` for context fields). Fixed by chat-plan normalization that coerces these generated context edges to `data` edges.
- **[21-6-C12] Validator `on_failure: retry` literal mismatch:** Validator schema accepts only `route|warn|halt`; generated plans sometimes used `retry`. Fixed by alias normalization in chat-plan preprocessing (`retry`→`route`, plus `continue/ignore`→`warn`, `stop/fail`→`halt`).
- **[21-6-C5] WebSocket "no close frame received or sent":** `chat_events_ws` in `app.py` broke out of the event loop on `None` sentinel but never called `await websocket.close()`, so no close frame was sent. Fixed by adding explicit close.
- **[21-6-I5] `dan-serve --reload` restarted on graph file changes:** Uvicorn reload watched the entire project root including `graphs/`. Creating `_scratch.json` (or any graph file save) triggered a server restart mid-stream. Fixed by excluding `graphs/`, `runs/`, and `checkpoints/` from `reload_excludes`.
- **[21-6-C1] `human_input_needed` silently dropped during `/run` from `dan-chat`:** `_CHAT_EVENT_TYPES` in `scoped_run.py` did not include `human_input_needed`, so `map_run_event_to_chat_block` returned `None` for these events. Any workflow with a HumanNode would hang silently when run via `/run` in `dan-chat`. Fixed by adding `human_input_needed` (and `run_cancelled`) to the set and adding mapping logic.
- **[22-3-T3] CLI `ChatClient.cancel_run()` route corrected:** Originally used `/api/runs/{id}/cancel` which doesn't exist; corrected to `POST /api/gateway/cancel` with body `{"run_id": run_id}`, matching `src/dan/client/client.py` (DanClient). Fixed during 21-6 implementation.
- **[21-6-C2] `dan-chat` REPL had no human input handler during `/run`:** Even if `human_input_needed` events reached the client, there was no code to prompt the user and POST the response back. Fixed by adding approval/text mode input handler (mirrors `dan-run`), plus `ChatClient.submit_human_input()` and `ChatClient.cancel_run()`.
- **[21-6-C3] `_pipe_run_events` hung 300s on `run_cancelled`:** Break condition only checked `run_completed` and `run_failed`, missing `run_cancelled`. A cancelled run would keep the WebSocket pipe open for 300s until the `asyncio.wait_for` timeout. Fixed by adding `run_cancelled` to the break condition.
- **[21-6-I4] `_run_after_approval` silently swallowed `start_run` exceptions:** If `start_run()` threw after plan approval (e.g., invalid graph, engine error), the exception propagated to the asyncio task which logged "Task exception was never retrieved" and the run stayed PENDING forever. Fixed by wrapping in `try/except` that sets `FAILED` status and emits `run_failed` event.
- **[R2-C1] TierScorer cache key collision across subgraphs:** Round 1 fix changed `id(graph)` to `(len(nodes), len(edges))`, but subgraphs with the same counts collide, sharing a scorer built for a different topology. Fixed by replacing with `_graph_fingerprint()` using sorted node/edge ID strings.
- **[R2-I1] Budget advisory mutated graph nodes:** `setattr(node, "target_input_tokens", advisory_tokens)` permanently modified nodes, persisting stale budget values across runs. Fixed by passing advisory through `inputs["__advisory_target_tokens__"]`.
- **[R2-I2] DIFF_BASED compaction no-op for AgentTeam:** `_apply_diff_based` returns same item count (replaces payloads with deltas), so `len(compacted) < len(msgs)` was always false. Fixed by adding `has_structural_changes` guard.
- **[R2-I3] SemanticCache non-atomic disk writes:** `path.write_text()` can produce truncated JSON on crash. Fixed with `tempfile.mkstemp` + `os.replace` atomic pattern.
- **[C1] `AgentTeamExecutor._apply_turn_compaction` crash:** `LoopCompactor.compact()` can produce synthetic dicts with `__summary__` or `__unchanged__` keys that lack the `sender`/`content` fields required by `TeamMessage(**d)`. Fixed by filtering `__unchanged__` entries and converting `__summary__` entries into system messages.
- **[I2] Fire-and-forget `ensure_future` in `run_manager.py`:** Four occurrences used deprecated `asyncio.get_event_loop()` + `ensure_future` with dead `else: loop.run_until_complete(...)` branches. Replaced with `get_running_loop().create_task()` + `_safe_async` wrapper for monitored exception handling.
- **[18-5] `_last_tier_result` concurrency hazard:** `ModelSelector._last_tier_result` was shared mutable state on a singleton; parallel `asyncio.gather` LLM nodes could overwrite each other's tier result before `LLMExecutor` read it. Fixed by returning tier result as part of `select()` return tuple.
- **[18-5] `tier_params` never wired:** `TierPolicy.tier_params` and `DEFAULT_TIER_PARAMS` were defined but `_select_tier()` never resolved them and `LLMExecutor` never applied them to provider calls. Fixed by resolving via `resolve_tier_params()` and threading through `_call_llm` → `_call_via_provider`.
- **[18-5] `_detect_providers` OpenAI false positive:** `getattr(config, "llm_api_key", "")` detected OpenAI even for Anthropic-only setups with a default API key, causing wrong tier map selection. Fixed by checking model name prefixes instead.
- **[14-*] Path traversal in `FileSystemMemoryStore`:** HTTP path params (`workflow_id`, `session_id`) were not sanitized, allowing `../` in IDs to escape the memory directory. Fixed by adding `_safe_path_segment()` sanitizer in `memory_store.py` and `_validate_path_segment()` regex guard in REST endpoints (`app.py`).
- **[14-2] `reads_global=[]` treated as full passthrough:** `ScopedContextView.__init__` used `if reads_global` (falsy for empty list), so an explicit empty allowlist was indistinguishable from `None` (no contract). Fixed by using `is not None` checks. Same for `writes_global`. Scheduler caller also fixed (`or None` → pass list directly).
- **[14-*] Memory plumbing dropped in subgraphs:** `_run_subgraph` called `_make_context` without forwarding `session_id`, `memory_writes`, or `short_term_memory`, so child subgraphs couldn't write memory or use the pipeline. Fixed by threading all three through `_run_subgraph` → `_make_context`.
- **[14-1] `WriteMode.DELETE` not handled:** `FileSystemMemoryStore.write()` ignored DELETE mode entries; `_flush_memory_writes` routed everything to a single `(workflow_id, session_id)` regardless of `MemoryScope`. Fixed: `write()` now delegates to `delete()` for DELETE mode; `_flush_memory_writes` routes GLOBAL to `_global/_global`, WORKFLOW to `<wf>/_default`, SESSION to `<wf>/<sess>`.
- **[14-2] Non-sticky signals emitted as global events:** `emit_signal()` fired a global `NODE_OUTPUT` engine event for both sticky and non-sticky signals, violating the "one level up" contract for non-sticky. Fixed: non-sticky signals now store in `LocalStateManager.__signals__` for parent consumption; only sticky signals emit global events.
- **[14-1] API memory dir divergence:** REST memory endpoints used `DAN_MEMORY_DIR` env var while engine used `EngineConfig.memory_dir`, potentially pointing to different directories. Fixed: `_get_memory_store()` now reads from `RunManager.engine_config.memory_dir`.
- **[14-1] Preloaded memory keys inaccessible via normal context reads:** Memory entries were injected only under `memory:<key>` prefix, making them invisible to context edges reading the raw key. Fixed: if the raw key is in `declared_keys`, it is written directly; the `memory:<key>` prefix is always written as well.

- **Code-node CSV writer could emit malformed rows on commas/quotes/newlines:** `write_csv.md` in vibe workflows manually built CSV lines with `",".join(...)`, which is not RFC-safe for arbitrary text fields. Fixed by switching v1/v2 `write_csv.md` to Python `csv.writer` and preserving pass-through outputs.
- **Parallel-phase copy race left stale tool nodes in vibe v2:** `examples/vibe_research_v2_md/plot_one.md` and `write_csv.md` were copied from v1 before Plan 7-5's migration landed, so v2 still referenced deprecated `tool_id` values (`plot_backtest`, `save_grid_csv`). Fixed by converting both v2 files to inline code nodes (matching v1 pattern) and using v2-specific output paths.
- **Nested drill-in broken beyond depth 1:** All navigation functions (`drillIn`, `drillOut`, `jumpToLayer`, `saveGraph`) used root-level `sub_graphs` lookup. Fixed by `resolveGraphAtStack` traversal helper + depth-3 cap. `PortMappingOverlay` parent lookup also fixed.
- **Port order non-deterministic:** Ports rendered in raw array order causing edge crossings. Fixed by `orderPorts` scoring: P0 gate pins → P1 connected (peer Y) → P2 unconnected (alpha).
- **`_rag_index_documents` sent `list[str]` to `Indexer.create_index` (expects `list[dict]` with `"text"` key):** Indexer silently failed to build a collection. Fixed by wrapping document content as `{"text": ..., "metadata": ...}`.
- **`_rag_index_documents` bypassed workspace sandboxing:** Absolute `pdf_dir` was resolved as-is via `Path(workspace) / pdf_dir`, escaping `DAN_WORKSPACE_ROOT`. Fixed by rejecting absolute paths and verifying the resolved path stays under the workspace root.
- **`TOOL_PORT_MANIFESTS` mismatched `compile_latex`/`save_paper`/`package_submission` signatures:** Manifests declared generic `input`/`tex_content` ports while tools required `title`, `content`, `tex_path`, `bib_path` as separate arguments. Caused `TypeError` at runtime. Fixed by aligning manifests with actual function signatures and adding structured output ports.
- **`data_ingest` pattern wired `list_directory` dict output into `rag_index_documents` string param:** `list_directory.result` is `{"entries": [...], "count": N}` but was connected to `pdf_dir` (string). Caused `TypeError`. Fixed by removing the intermediate `list_directory` node — `rag_index_documents` does its own file discovery internally.
- **`informs_paper_writing` template implicitly auto-created `context` port on Outline Planner:** `add_edge` targeting a nonexistent port generated a diagnostic warning and contradicted strict-mode hardening. Fixed by declaring explicit `input_ports` config on the Outline Planner node.
- **Flow parser crashed on non-integer `parallel`/`max` kwargs:** `each()`, `loop()`, and `parallel()` called `int()` directly on kwargs without catching `ValueError`. Fixed by wrapping in try/except and raising `FlowParseError` with the offending value.
- **Editor ConfigPanel: clearing input mapping text deleted the row:** `updateInputMapping` deleted the mapping when inner port was empty string, causing the input row to vanish mid-edit. Fixed by always storing the value; deletion is via the explicit `×` button.
- **Loader silently accepted unknown merge strategy in parallel():** An unrecognized `merge:` value (e.g. `merge: unknown`) silently defaulted to APPEND. Fixed by emitting a diagnostic error listing valid strategies.
- **Decompiler `_invert_until_condition` could truncate trailing expressions:** `not (A) or B` was incorrectly stripped to `A`. Fixed by checking for trailing content after the closing paren; if present, the original string is returned unchanged.
- **Markdown loop decompile/compile could invert semantics and drop loop state:** Fixed in Plan 7-8 — decompiler now inverts `gate.condition` back to `until` (strips one `not (...)` layer) and emits `state:`/`defaults:` when present. Round-trip preserves semantics.
- **ContextEdge read permission validation checked source node, while runtime reads on target node:** Fixed in Plan 7-8 — `_check_context_edge_permissions` now validates `target_node_id`'s `read_set` for READ mode (consumer); WRITE/APPEND still validate source.
- **Gate while-mode could be miswired by default chain/f-string wiring:** Fixed in Plan 7-8 — builder `default_output_port` is now mode-aware: `gate_mode="while"` → `"continue"`, `gate_mode="if_else"` → `"true"`.
- **Flow parse failures were warnings (not fatal) in markdown compiler:** Fixed in Plan 7-8 — `compile_workflow(path, strict=True)` treats parse_warnings as errors; ambiguous bare-edge also fails in strict mode.
- **Mutator auto-created missing target ports silently:** Fixed in Plan 7-8 — mutator now adds diagnostic on auto-create; optional `strict=True` on add_edge fails instead of auto-creating.
- **Generated custom strategy code was not persisted for reproducibility:** Workflow could execute custom `build_factor` code but did not save the script artifact, making exact reruns hard. Fixed by saving scripts before execution to `examples/vibe_research_md/output/scripts/{strategy}.py` in `_run_strategy_script`, and propagating `script_saved_path`/`script_save_error` through `bug_fixer` and `save_tracking` outputs.
- **Sub-$1 stocks were included at portfolio formation in vibe backtests:** Formation universes were not consistently filtered by price, so penny stocks could enter quintile sorting. Fixed by enforcing `|price| >= 1` in `quant_lib` backtests: built-in momentum/reversal now carry formation price and filter directly; custom-factor backtests auto-attach CRSP price by `(date, permno)` when missing and apply the same threshold.
- **`skip` gate wiring suppressed plots on non-skip runs (factors produced, no figures):** `department.md` routed `dept_gate.if(skip).true` into `bug_fixer`, and scheduler skip semantics treat missing gate branch data as inactive. On normal `skip=false` runs, this could skip `bug_fixer` before backtest outputs arrived, so factors were saved but plotting never executed. Fixed by isolating halt routing to `dept_halt.md` and making `bug_fixer` return `{}` when no backtest payload is present.
- **Generated custom scripts crashed with `incompatible tolerance <DateOffset: months=...>` in `merge_asof`:** Some LLM scripts pass `pd.DateOffset` as `merge_asof(tolerance=...)`, which pandas rejects for datetime64 join keys. Fixed in `_run_strategy_script` safe merge wrapper by normalizing/removing incompatible tolerance and retrying.
- **Custom build scripts could print internal errors and return empty factors, causing “factors but no figures” confusion:** Generated `build_factor` code sometimes catches internal exceptions and returns an empty-but-schema-valid DataFrame after printing diagnostics. Runtime used to persist/backtest these empties, producing no plot series. Fixed by capturing `build_factor` stdout/stderr in `_run_strategy_script` and hard-failing empty factor outputs before save/backtest.
- **Department composite output shape mismatched merge expectations:** `department.md` exits through `save_tracking`, but merge logic consumed department outputs as backtest dicts. This caused empty/partial downstream results visibility even when factor/backtest work ran. Fixed by making `save_tracking` return passthrough backtest fields (`strategy_name`, `quintiles`, spread, series, plot metadata) in addition to persistence paths.
- **`max_factors` appeared ignored (many factors generated when set to 2):** Stop logic only counted successful `results`, so failed attempts could continue until safety-cap iterations and still emit many factor files. Fixed by capping on attempted strategies too (`len(strategies_tried) >= max_factors`) in `governor.md`.
- **Factors existed but no immediate machine-readable results file:** Department flow produced factors/plots but lacked per-factor result artifacts for quick inspection. Fixed by writing `output/results/{strategy}.json` and `output/latest_result_{dept}.json` in `save_tracking.md` after each backtest.
- **Backtest/plot visibility lagged until end-of-run:** Top-level plotting and CSV export happened after the while-loop `done`, so users could not see progress as factors completed. Fixed by plotting inside `bug_fixer` per factor and updating `tracking_{dept}.json` immediately after each backtest via `save_tracking(backtest_result=...)`.
- **Departments backtested in parallel when one-by-one debugging was needed:** Iteration executed MOM/REV/FUND in parallel fan-out. Fixed by serializing department assignment flow with dependency gates (`MOM -> REV -> FUND`) so each department backtest completes before the next starts.
- **FUND/custom strategies produced no persisted factor files:** Builtin backtests saved factor parquet files via CLI `--save-factor-dir`, but custom `run_strategy_script` path only returned backtest metrics and did not persist factors. Fixed by saving `factor_df` to `examples/vibe_research_md/output/factors/<strategy_name>.parquet` and returning `factor_saved_path` (plus non-fatal `factor_save_error`).
- **Generated custom scripts failed on `name 'ValueError' is not defined`:** Sandbox builtins only exposed `NameError` and `Exception`, so standard `except ValueError` patterns in generated code crashed. Fixed by adding common exception classes (`ValueError`, `TypeError`, `KeyError`, `IndexError`, `AttributeError`) to `_ALLOWED_BUILTINS`.
- **Raw `pd.merge_asof` in generated code still failed with `left keys must be sorted`:** Even with prompt guidance, generated code could call unsorted `pd.merge_asof`. Fixed in `_run_strategy_script` by temporarily monkey-patching `pd.merge_asof` to a safe sorting wrapper during code execution/build_factor invocation.
- **Legacy Compustat names in stale tracking params broke FUND runs (`ceq` vs `ceqq`, `at` vs `atq`):** Fixed by adding backward-compat alias columns in `load_compustat()` and normalizing common param aliases before `build_factor` execution.
- **merge→governor dropped `new_strategies` port:** The bare edge `merge → governor` hit the compiler's ambiguity path (two matching ports: `new_results`, `new_strategies`) and only wired the first alphabetically. `strategies_tried` never accumulated, causing the orchestrator to repeat strategies. Fixed with explicit `merge.new_results → governor.new_results` + `merge.new_strategies → governor.new_strategies`.
- **dept_gate.theme never reached strategy_manager:** Orchestrator generated per-iteration themes but `department.md` never wired `dept_gate.theme` to `strategy_manager.theme`. Strategy manager operated without theme context. Fixed by adding the edge and a `{theme}` reference in the prompt.
- **dept_gate.skip unused — halted departments ran full pipeline:** `dept_gate` set `skip=true` when `action=="halt"`, but no gate consumed it. Halted departments still made LLM calls and ran backtests. Fixed by adding `dept_gate | if("skip", then: bug_fixer, else: load_tracking)` to short-circuit halted departments.
- **Builtin backtest always ran momentum, ignoring reversal:** `run_backtest()` hardcoded `build_momentum()` and `strategy_name="momentum_..."` regardless of `factor_name`. REV department computed 1-month past return but never negated it. Fixed: negate signal for reversal, label correctly, pass `factor_type` through item.
- **Merge silently dropped all department results (REV, FUND, and even MOM):** `merge.md` looked for `c.get("backtest_result")` and `c.get("strategy_id")`, but department composites output the backtest dict directly (keys: `quintiles`, `strategy_name`, `spread_q5_q1_bps`, etc.) — no nesting. So `new_results` and `new_strategies` were always `[]`, governor accumulated nothing, and the loop ran until max iterations with an empty results list. Fixed merge to treat each department input as the backtest result itself and use `strategy_name`.
- **LLM-generated code used wrong Compustat column names (`at` instead of `atq`, `ceq` instead of `ceqq`, etc.)**: Fixed — strategy coder prompt now includes full quarterly column reference from user-provided DATA-DICTIONARY.md. `load_compustat()` and `safe_merge_asof()` injected into sandbox namespace. Strategy manager prompt updated with concrete column examples per department theme.
- **Custom strategy scripts crashed with `KeyError: ['ret']`**: Fixed — `load_crsp()` now normalizes a canonical `ret` alias (from `return_month` or `return_ex_div_month`) and keeps `mktcap`/`market_cap_month` aliases aligned. This matches strategy-coder contract and prevents `build_factor(...).dropna(subset=["ret"])` failures.
- **Vibe run crashed when saving checkpoints (`ValueError: Circular reference detected`)**: Fixed — removed self-referential strategy output (`out["result"] = out`) from `_run_strategy_script`, so checkpoint JSON serialization no longer encounters recursive dictionaries.
- **Generated strategy code failed with `NameError: name 'next' is not defined`**: Fixed — added `iter` and `next` to the sandbox/code executor allowed builtins used by `execute_python()`.
- **`Engine.run(inputs=...)` lost on InputNode variables**: Fixed — scheduler now injects virtual run inputs into `InputNode.variables` (not just `input_ports`), so workflow inputs like `start_year/end_year/max_factors` propagate correctly.
- **While loop body ran before initial gate continue signal**: Fixed — skip logic for `gate.continue/loop` now only bypasses gate-source checks when virtual loop-feedback inputs are present, preventing premature first-pass body execution.
- **Drop position wrong when zoomed/panned**: Fixed — replaced manual `clientX - bounds.left` with `screenToFlowPosition()` from `useReactFlow()` in `GraphCanvas.tsx`.
- **Node ID collisions after page refresh**: Fixed — replaced module-level `_counter` with `Date.now()` + random suffix in `graphAdapter.ts`.
- **Delete key doesn't fire reliably**: Fixed — removed wrapper `onKeyDown` handler, added `deleteKeyCode={["Delete", "Backspace"]}` prop to `<ReactFlow>` in `GraphCanvas.tsx`.
- **`onConnect` creates mismatched edge IDs**: Fixed — extracted `Date.now()` to a single `edgeId` const in `useGraphStore.ts`.
- **`hasBodyGraph` in App.tsx uses useCallback, should be useMemo**: Fixed — changed to `useMemo` returning a boolean, updated call site from `hasBodyGraph()` to `hasBodyGraph`.
- **`Object.groupBy` compatibility in `NodePalette`**: Fixed — replaced `Object.groupBy` with a typed `reduce` grouping implementation, removing ES2024 dependency.
- **Read-only drill-in bypassable**: Fixed — `NodePalette` and `ConfigPanel` now disable all add/edit/drag actions when `layerStack.length > 0`.
- **Template port contract mismatch**: Fixed — LLM template output port renamed to `text` (matching executor), ReAct condition to `True`, Plan-Execute given proper port mappings.
- **`startRun()` proceeds after failed save**: Fixed — `saveGraph()` returns boolean, `startRun` aborts if `false`.
- **Edge type edit doesn't update styling**: Fixed — `updateEdgeData` syncs top-level `animated`, `label`, `style.stroke` when `edge_type` changes.
- **Subgraph events lack hierarchy tags**: Fixed — `layer_path` threaded through `ExecutionContext` and emitted with every event.
- **LogPanel shows node IDs instead of names**: Fixed — `nodeNameMap` reads `data.name` (not `data.label`).
- **Paper workflow LaTeX compile fails on missing INFORMS assets/citation keys**: Fixed — `compile_latex` now auto-fetches `informs3.cls`, normalizes TeX for `plainnat` compatibility (`hyperref`, `\newblock`), and auto-fills missing BibTeX keys with placeholder entries.

## Known Limitations

- **reactFlowEdgeToDan can drop edge metadata (11-3):** When `rfEdge.data?.danEdge` is absent, the adapter creates a default DataEdge with `edge_type: "data"`, losing `edge_type` (control/context), `spread`, `context_key`, `mode`, `condition`. Ensure editor-created edges always preserve `danEdge` on `data`.
- **DEFAULT_OUTPUT_PORTS inconsistency (11-1):** Builder vs mutator disagree on composite (`result` vs `output`) and while_loop (`result` vs `output`). May cause port mismatches when mutator adds nodes. Future: extract to shared `dan.models.ports` or `dan.constants`.
- **TS NodeBase lacks read_set/write_set (11-1):** Python `NodeBase` has `read_set`/`write_set` for context declarations; `editor/src/types/graph.ts` omits them. Round-trip and ContextEdge validation may lose fidelity. Add to TS interfaces for parity.
- **Loader feature gaps (11-3):** Markdown loader emits only DataEdge; no ControlEdge or ContextEdge. Loader cannot define RAG, Validator, Reduce nodes. Round-trip via markdown loses control/context edges, artifact_refs, loop_groups. Use Python/JSON canonical source for full fidelity.
- **No graph validation in editor**: The frontend saves graphs without running the Python validation rules (`validate_graph`). Invalid graphs (missing entry points, broken edges) can be saved and will fail at run time. Validation feedback in the editor is a future enhancement.
- **Single-user only**: The run manager holds state in memory. Multiple browser tabs can connect to the same run via WebSocket, but there's no multi-user session isolation.
- ~~**No error feedback — silent API failures**~~: Resolved in Phase 3.5 — toast notification system added; all store actions wrapped in try/catch with error toasts.
- ~~**No loading states**~~: Resolved in Phase 3.5 — `loadingGraph`/`savingGraph` flags with spinners in `EditorToolbar`.
- ~~**No connection validation**~~: Resolved in Phase 3.5 — `isValidConnection` callback added in `GraphCanvas` (rejects self-connections, duplicates).
- **ConfigPanel is a generic field dump**: Iterates all non-skipped fields without logical grouping. JSON fields have a raw textarea with silent `JSON.parse` catch — no validation feedback. No way to add/remove/rename ports. (Partially improved: edge config is now editable with type-specific fields.)
- ~~**Fixed-height bottom panel**~~: Resolved in Phase 3.5 — resizable panels via `allotment`.
- ~~**Two header bars waste vertical space**~~: Resolved in Phase 3.5 — merged into single `EditorToolbar`.
- ~~**No keyboard shortcuts**~~: Resolved in Phase 3.5 — Cmd+S save shortcut added.
- **No undo/redo**: React Flow supports undo/redo via state snapshots, but it's not wired up yet.
- **App title/favicon are Vite defaults**: HTML title is "editor", favicon is `vite.svg`. Basic branding gap.
- **Node.js version warning**: Vite 7.x and `@vitejs/plugin-react` require Node.js 20.19+ or 22.12+. Current dev environment runs 20.17 — builds succeed with warnings but may break on future Vite updates. Fix: upgrade Node.js.
- ~~**`dan-up` kills slow-starting server (25s timeout)**~~: Fixed — timeout increased to 60s and timeout no longer kills the server process if it's still alive. Server startup also reduced from ~33s to ~1s by deferring memory kernel imports to background threads.

## Phase 7 Review Fixes (2026-02-25)

- **Chat mutation events silently dropped**: Fixed — `ChatPanel.tsx` now handles `chat_mutation` WebSocket events.
- **`/run` chat commands ignored**: Fixed — `app.py::chat_message` now dispatches to `parse_run_command` + `build_scoped_graph`.
- **Token usage keys mismatched (backend→frontend)**: Fixed — `_normalize_usage()` maps `prompt_tokens`/`completion_tokens` → `prompt`/`completion`.
- **`_chat_streams` memory leak**: Fixed — entries now carry monotonic timestamps; stale entries reaped on each POST.
- **Markdown link XSS via `javascript:` URLs**: Fixed — `ChatMessage.tsx` allowlists `https:`, `http:`, `mailto:`, `#`.
- **Mention autocomplete fires mid-word**: Fixed — `findMentionQuery` requires whitespace or start-of-input before `@`.
- **Workflow mention click was no-op**: Fixed — `navigateToMention` calls `store.openTab()`.

Restricted `agy models` could not create cache/log files; the authorized normal launch succeeded and reported sign-in required.

All non-archived project rows now get controls and register their complete group on action; stale profile IDs fall back to matching recorded folder roots.

Cause is not established; do not claim Claude authenticated acceptance. Antigravity awaits first CLI sign-in. Protocol and delegation tests use fixtures.

- Dev (`localhost:5173`) and packaged (`127.0.0.1:45173`) origins hold separate workspace registries. Switching to the packaged app exposed internal workflow IDs and resurrected hidden-project groups despite intact backend histories. Recovered the original registry from the existing Electron profile and restored it to the packaged origin. Shared server-side registry remains backlog; do not mistake missing UI metadata for deleted sessions.

## 2026-09-18 — Transcript selection disappearing
- Quote toolbar updates recreated the Markdown inner-HTML prop object, replacing text nodes and clearing selection. Memoize the object and avoid follow-scroll while selecting. Regression tests and Chromium checks pass.

- **Fixed 2026-09-18: native response truncated at 500 characters.** Completion summaries replaced full streamed text. Preserve full text when the summary is its prefix and restore affected native messages using the matching completed run result on reopen.

- **GitHub authentication (2026-09-20):** earlier API checks returned 401 despite SSH source access. Browser device authorization restored API access as 2Mars4096. SSH Git transport and release API authentication are separate; do not suggest changing repository visibility to resolve this.

- **Local updater packaging (2026-09-20), fixed:** electron-builder `--dir --publish never` did not emit `app-update.yml` for the prepared local client. Explicit `build/github-update.yml` extraResources now carries the private GitHub channel/cache metadata in local app bundles as well as release builds.
